(2) The increase in surplus reserve was because the Company provided 10% of the net profit of 2008 as statutory surplus reserve of 2008.

(3) The change in undistributed profit was due to the increase of net profit of the reporting period, which increased net undistributed profit of 466,130,000 yuan.

The Company provided statutory surplus reserve of 46,610,000 yuan, provision for general risk of 194,680,000 yuan and dividend of common shares for 2008 of 102,320,000 yuan, which decreased undistributed profit of 343,610,000 yuan. Eventually, the undistributed profit increased 12,251,000 yuan.

## V. Non-performing loans at the end of the reporting period and the actions we should take

During the reporting period, because of the severe influence on tangible economy brought by macro-economy turning down and the sluggish international market, the non-performing loan of the Company increased. At the end of the reporting period, the balance of non-performing loan according to the five-class classification system was 297,900,000 yuan, increasing 170,180,000 yuan compared with that at the beginning of the year. The non-performing loan ratio was 1.45%, increasing by 0.70%. During the reporting period, the Company adopted a series of effective approaches suppressing the increase of non-performing loan: The first was actively responding to the macro-economic regulation of the government, tightening the standards of loan and credit approval, carrying out predictive withdrawal system and optimizing the loan asset structure. The second was reinforcing and regulating business operation management and strictly carrying out the loan examination system (i.e. Pre-loan-issuance investigation, loan issuance examination and post-loan-issuance examination). The third was making more efforts to collecting and disposing non-performing loans and specifying the key points of loan collection; reinforcing the collection of new overdue loans and effectively collecting non-performing loans. The fourth was establishing a multi-level organization structure, specifying the responsibilities of each level and collecting and disposing non-performing loans in a centralized manner.

#### (viii) Risk management over credit approval for group customers

During the reporting period, the Company formulated the Management Rules on Credit Approval for Group Customers, persisting in the unified, moderate and precaution' principles: The first was managing and controlling the risk of credit approval for group customers and in a centralized manner. In principle, branches

(sub-branches) would approve the credit to all related companies of the group customer. The second was setting an overall credit line to the group customer, which was no more than 15% of the equity of the Company. The third was closely examining the customer's condition after the loan was issued and learning the related information and analyzing the business situation of the group customer through various channels to prevent and eliminate the credit risk of group customers.

Unit: RMB '000 yuan

Notes: (1) Bank acceptance bill is a credit loan business in which the payee or payer (or cashing applicant) issues a commercial acceptance bill and the cashing applicant applies to the Bank and the Bank agrees to cash the commercial acceptance bill after examination.

(2) L/G is a credit loan business in which the Bank is requested by the applicant or authorized person to issue a letter of guarantee promising to the beneficiary that when the applicant fails to fulfill the liabilities and undertakings set in the contract, the Bank will cover the debt or bear the liabilities as ruled in the letter of guarantee.

(3) L/C is a credit loan in which the Bank is requested and instructed by the applicant to issue a written guarantee letter of certain amount to the beneficiary which guarantees to pay the amount in a given period at a given place upon given documents.

(4) Loan undertakings refers to that after negotiation with the customer, the Bank issues a letter of undertaking to the customer undertaking to issue a certain amount of loan to the customer in a certain period.

##### (ii) Off-balance-sheet risk management

During the reporting period, the Company included off-balance-sheet credit authorization into the centralized credit approval management scope and controlled and prevented its risks in line with the rules on general credit and loan management. The Company set the controlling percentages for total off-balance-sheet open and acceptance bill open to the total loan balance. The Company also limited the total size of the acceptance bill open and the balance of face value.

## XVI. Risks and Countermeasures of the Company

The Company faced credit risk, liquidity risk, market risk and operating risk. Recognizing these risks fully, the Company had engaged in improving risk management ability. The Company established an effective risk management system and prudent risk management culture, formulated Guidance for Total Risk Management of Bank of Wenzhou, kept optimizing the risk management organizational structure and the policies and process for risk management. By using advanced risk measurement and management tools, the Company tried to realize growth while keeping high assets quality. In the reporting period, no risks that imposed material influences to the business operation of the Company occurred.

#### (i) Credit Risk Management

Credit risk refers to the possibility of loss and uncertainty of income of the bank due to the customer's breach of agreement or deterioration of credit in the commercial bank's credit, loan and investment business. The credit risk of the Bank mainly exists in the on-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet business including loan, inter-bank lending, bond investment, bill acceptance, L/C, and letter of indemnity of the bank.

During the reporting period, the Company followed the principle of carrying out the macroeconomic regulation policy, targeted to improving the risk management ability and focused on adjusting asset structure and improving asset quality to promote risk management and ensure all businesses of the Company to develop in a balanced, orderly and healthy way.

According to the requirement of the macroeconomic regulation policy, the Company tightened credit approval standards to control the growth of loan. The Company balanced loan issue to avoid negative influence of the adjustment of industrial policy and the periodical industrial fluctuation on the loan and improve the efficiency of the loan capital.

By reinforcing policy guidance to credit policy and controlling over credit approval, the Company actively adjusted loan issuing direction and optimized credit structure. During the credit approving process, the Company provided green channel for loans to industrial sectors supported by the government, restricted the credit approving object and total credit amount for loans to industrial sectors regulated by the government, and formulated cutting-down and withdrawing approaches for loans to industrial sectors restricted by the government so as to reinforce structure adjustment and control from the source.

By reinforcing active countermeasures such as early risk warning, reinforcing post-loan examination and making more efforts in collecting and disposing existing and new non-performing loans, the Company controlled and relieved credit risk.

#### (ii) Liquidity Risk Management

Liquidity risk refers to the risk due to unable to cash the assets at a reasonable price in order to provide fund for due debts. The liquidity risks of the Company are mainly resulted from activities providing funds such as loans, transactions and investment as well as management over current assets.

By internal capital transferring system, the Company manages liquidity of the bank in a centralized manner. In the aspect of assets, the Company keeps a property percentage of short-term assets such as bill discount asset and highly liquid inter-bank lending, repurchase agreement and investment in central bank bills to ensure adequate liquidity. In the aspect of liabilities, the Company raises money from various channels including the public market of the central bank, inter-bank monetary market and bill market of the financial industry to ensure financing channels unblocked and daily liquidity demand satisfied. The Company sets various percentage standards (including but not limited to deposit/loan ratio, standby deposit ratio and setting up instructive ratio goal for every branches and sub-branches) and limit to transaction amount according to the overall asset and liability status in order to monitor and manage liquidity risk. The Company has also established the early warning system for liquidity risk and the liquidity management system to monitor the fund balance of each branch or sub-branch every day in

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


order to keep safe liquidity.

During the reporting period, the Company reinforced asset and liability management in time under the monetary policy and regulatory requirements of the regulatory authority and according to the growth of the deposit and loan of the Company to control the liquidity effectively. In general, the liquidity of the Company kept stable. At the end of the reporting period, the positive liquidity gap of the Company was 1.129 billion yuan. The liquidity gap for real-time repayment was -6.656 billion yuan, which was because the Company had a high amount of deposit that would be mature in three months and a high amount of deposit on call, accounting for 60.55% of the deposit of customers. Therefore, liquidity risk of real-time repayment was actually low.

##### (iii) Market Risk Management

Market risk refers to the risk of loss on the on-balance-sheet and off-balance sheet transactions of the bank due to negative changes in market price (interest rate, foreign exchange rate, stock price and commodity price). The Board of the Company authorized the Risk Management Committee to manage the overall market risk of the Company. The Risk Management Committee gives opinions and advice to the Board on the approval of the strategies and policies for the market risk management and in deciding the market risk level the Company can bear, urges the management team to take necessary actions to identify, measure, monitor and control market risks, acquires regular reports on market risk type and level, monitors and assesses the completeness and effectiveness of market risk management and diligence of the management team in market risk management. The market risk of the Company mainly includes interest rate risk and foreign exchange risk.

The market risk measurement methods of the Company include but not limited to VAR, gap analysis, mark to market, duration analysis, sensitivity analysis, pressure test and post hoc test. The company has established regular market risk analysis reporting system and provisional major event reporting system and Market Risk Management Department informs the management team and broad of directors about the latest market risk status in time.

### 1. Interest rate risk

Interest rate risk refers to the risk of negative influence on the financial status of the Company due to the fluctuation of the market interest rate. The interest rate risk of the Company is mainly caused by the incompliance of the terms of asset and liability transactions and the influence on the equity of the Company from the fluctuation on of market interest rate. According to the changes in the market interest rate and the reality of the Company, the Company adjusts the duration of assets and liabilities in time, scientifically and properly. The Company is targeted to keep the steady growth of net interest income in short term and to increase the equity of the Company steadily in long term to reduce interest rate risk.

### 2. Foreign exchange rate risk

Foreign exchange rate refers to the risk of negative influence on the financial status due to the fluctuation of the market foreign exchange rate. The foreign exchange risk of the Company is mainly derived from foreign currency open position. In view of the increasing international financial market unrest, the severe fluctuation of the exchange rate of main currencies and the increasingly big two-way fluctuation of CNY, the Company followed rational, long-term and prudential principles, carried out balanced management over assets and liabilities, actively adjusted the structure assets and liabilities in foreign currencies and optimized internal risk control system to lower the risk of open position and the risk of foreign exchange rate.



#### (iv) Operating Risk Management

Operating risk refers to the risk of loss caused by imperfect or problematic internal process, staff and IT system and external affairs. During the reporting period, the Company did not have any major operating risk accidents which would damage the reputation of the Company. To further improve internal control management and prevent operating risk, the Company made more efforts in carrying out ISO 9000 quality management system and the internal control system. By sorting out business and management process, the Company identified and assessed various operating risks, worked out controlling approaches and established a systematic, transparent and document-based internal control system. Via the IT platform, the Company sent internal control documents to every staff member of the Company in time and effectively to carry out them.

The Company kept optimizing operating risk management structure, system platform and technology approaches to further improve operating risk management ability. Within the total risk management frame, the Company made clear of the definition, classification of operating risks and the environment construction for the operation mechanism of the operating risk management. During the reporting period, catching the opportunities of building the internal control system and based on handling specialized cases, the Company actively carried out risk management examination, assessed the internal control status and operating risk status of the Company objectively and developed operating risk management culture. Meanwhile, the Company improved operating risk-controlling ability by improving the IT system.

##### (v) Management of other risks

### 1. Legal risk

The Company has established a complete and normative legal risk management organization structure, has a clear legal affair management process and created a legal affair management team making up of legal professionals. It is to effectively control the legal risk of various businesses. The legal management department provided positive and effective legal assurance for the Company's lawsuits risk control, examination of document and agreement with external parties, business management authorization and various social and economic activities participated by the Company. It effectively reduced legal risk. With the improvement of the awareness of legal and conforming business operations, the legal management department has become a necessary player in launching new products and services and assessing the legal risk and related management rules and operation process in traditional business innovation. The Company has gradually established related co-signing and examination system.

### 2. Strategy risk

The Company studied macroeconomic policy and regional financial environment development trend in depth and integrated the market positioning of being based on local economy, based on small and middle enterprises and

based on residents in cities and countryside and the strategic direction of being an outstanding retail bank with regional market strength with the macroeconomic and financial policy of expanding domestic demand, promoting reform and development of countryside and ensuring the steady development of SMEs. The strategic direction set out by the Company was based on the direction of economic and financial development and the development requirement of the Company and depended on stable customers, good financial status and proper regional distribution. The strategies of the Company are able to adapt to the business environment changes in China. The Company is able to execute the strategies well and the strategy risk is controlled in a low level.

## XVII.Notes to Completeness, Reasonableness and Validity of Internal Control

Following the Company Act of the People's Republic of China, the Commercial Bank Act of the People's Republic of China, Guidance for Internal Control of Commercial Banks, Guidance for Corporate Governance of Joint-Stock Commercial Banks and integrating the advanced management modes of domestic and overseas commercial banks, the Company optimized the internal organization structure and has established a corporate governance frame consisting of Shareholders' Meeting, Board of Directors, Supervisory Board and the top management and realized the operating system that ownership, operating right and supervising right are separated and inter-restrictive. The Company introduced in and passed ISO9000 quality management system certification, established and optimized controlling systems for authorization and approval, power and responsibility separation, credit and loan risk control, accounting system control, information system control and assets in kind control, which are all carried out effectively. Oriented by total risk management, the Company reinforced credit risk, liquidity risk, market risk and operating risk management. The internal audit department assessed the internal control status of the headquarters and branches annually, monitored and assessed the business activities constantly, reported to the management and the Board in writing and disclosed defects in internal control and gave suggestions on improvement in the report. The Company recognized, measured and recorded valid economic transactions pursuant to rules and regulations. The advanced computer software and internal network system provided technology support for reflecting various kinds of business information timely and correctly. The financial report of 2008 properly disclosed the financial status, business outcomes and cash flow of the year. Smooth communications between the management and the internal departments and external departments were ensured, including communications with related interested parties, regulatory departments and CPAs. The chief accountant designation system of the Company could ensure branch institutions recording various transactions timely, correctly and completely.

#### (ii) Shares issued in the past three years before the end of the reporting period

According to ZYJF (2006) No.44 Approval of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank Modifying Stock Capital and Related Matters issued by China Banking Regulatory Commission Zhejiang Regulatory Bureau, WSYD [2005] No.28 Resolution to Employee Share Subscription of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, and WSYD [2006] No.29 Resolution to Shude Group and Other Nine Companies Holding Shares of the Bank, the Company offered additional 486,183,800 shares in 2006. The price was 1.065 yuan per share.

Approved by File ZYJF (2006) No.44 of China Banking Regulatory Commission Zhejiang Regulatory Bureau, the Company finished the change of business registration on June 30, 2006. The total stock capital increased from 470,795,800 shares to 1,007,213,531 shares.

Approved by File ZYJF [2006] No.107 of China Banking Regulatory Commission Zhejiang Regulatory Bureau, the Company finished the change of business registration on December 31, 2006. The total stock capital increased from 1,007,213,531 shares to 1,023,181,921 shares.

## ☐ VI. Directors, Supervisors, High-Rank Managerial Staff and Staff

I. Information of directors, supervisors and high-rank managerial staff

II. Work experiences and positions of current directors, supervisors and high-rank managerial staff

##### (i) Directors

Xia Ruizhou, male, born in November 1946, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be Deputy Secretary of the district CCP Committee and Head of Luofu District, Yongjia County; Wenzhou; secretary of the CCP Committee of Qiaotou Town and member of the Yongjia County CCP Committee; Secretary of the CCP Committee of Oubei Town, member of the Yongjia County CCP Committee; Deputy Secretary of Wencheng County CCP Committee and Head of Wencheng County; Deputy Director of the Incorporation Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Chairman of Bank of Wenzhou.

Xing Zengfu, male, born in October 1963, master degree and senior accountant, used to be Vice President of ICBC Linhai Sub-Branch; President of ICBC Jiaojiang Sub-Branch; Director of the Business Department and Peony Card Department of ICBC Taizhou Branch; Chairman and General Manager of Sino-Foreign Joint Venture Zhejiang Guangsha Property Co., Vice President of ICBC Wenzhou Branch. Currently, he is President and Vice Chairman of Bank of Wenzhou.

Huang Chenyuan, male, born in April 1954, junior college degree, senior economist, used to be Chief of staff of the navy, Director of the Government Bond Office of Wenzhou Public Finance Bureau, Deputy Director of the Incorporation Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, Assistant to President of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Director and Vice President of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zhao Yiyuan, male, born in October 1964, master school degree, senior (certified) accountant and lawyer, used to be Chairman and Chief accountant of Wenzhou Huaming CPAs Firm. Currently, he is Chairman of Wenzhou Huaming Capital Operation Consultation Co., Ltd., Executive Director of Wenzhou Jinruniu Frozen Food Co., Ltd. and an independent director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zhao Xiaolei, male, born in May 1955, Ph.D and professor, used to be Vice Director of the Financial and Economic Research Institute of Shanghai University of Finance and Economics, doctoral student supervisor and Director of the Publicity Department of the CCP Committee of Shanghai University of Finance and Economics. Currently, he is Director of the Financial and Economic Research Institute of Shanghai University of Finance and Economics, doctoral student supervisor of Shanghai University of Finance and Economics and an independent director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Chen Chaohui, male, born in May 1963, Ph.D and professor, used to be a research of Guangzhou Economic Research Institute, lecturer of EU Economics, Jean Monnet and Researcher of Financial Market Research Center, London School of Economics and Political Science, Vice President of JPMorgan, Director of the First Light Academy, U.S., CEO of Global Education Management Co., Ltd. (China). Currently, he is Director of Shanghai First Light Financial Management College and Independent Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Lin Qiliang, male, born in May 1950, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be Chief of the Personnel and Secretary Division of the People's Bank of China Wenzhou Chengxi Office and Vice President of Ouhai Sub-branch (in charge); Director of ICBC Wenzhou Branch Jiushan Office, Director of the Audit Department of Wenzhou Branch, President of Wenzhou Branch Lucheng Sub-branch, Chief Auditor of Wenzhou Branch and Director of the Office of Shareholding System Reform. Currently, he is an independent director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Shan Huaiguang, male, bron in December 1941, undergraduate degree and senior engineer, used to be Project Manager of No.9 Lab of the Institute of Computing Technology of Chinese Academy of Science, Manager of the Computer Dept. and Development Dept. of Bank of China Group Hong Kong; Chief of the Computer Department of Bank of China Nanjing Branch (under preparation); General Manager of the Technology Dept. and General Manager of Overseas Center of ICBC, Chief Technology Advisor of China Union pay. Currently, he is President of Data Service Company of China Union Pay and an independent director of Bank of Wenzhou.



Xu Mengzhou, male, born in September 1950, graduate degree and professor, used to be a professor of the Law School of Renmin University of China. Currently, he is a professor of the Law School of Renmin University of China, Vice Director of the Financial Law Research Institute and an independent director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Sun Jianwei, male, born in June 1962, undergraduate degree and accountant, used to be director of Agricultural Finance Dept, of Wenzhou Municipal Public Finance and Taxation Bureau, and Director of Wenzhou Municipal Local Taxation Bureau Lucheng Branch. Currently, he is Vice Director of Wenzhou Public Finance Bureau and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Hu Ying, female, born in January 1965, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be vice president of ICBC Quhua Sub-Branch (in charge), Manager, Senior Assistant to Manager, Senior Vice Manager and Senior Manager of China Huarong Asset Management Company Hangzhou Office. Currently, she is Vice General Manager of China Huarong Asset Management Company Hangzhou Office and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Chen Shengquan, male, born in August 1964, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be Director of Wenzhou Dongyu Power Plan Electric Branch, Vice Manager of Wenzhou Electricity Industrial Corp. Electricity Inspection and Maintenance Branch; General Manager of China-foreign joint venture Wenzhou Hongneng Electricity Co., Ltd., and General Manager of Zhejiang Transformer Co., Ltd. Currently, he is General Manager of Zhejiang Changtai Technology Co., Ltd., Vice General Manager (in charge) of Changtai Holdings Group Co., Ltd. and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zheng Nianhong, male, born in May 1951, junior college degree and senior accountant, used to be Financial Director of Wenzhou Ceramic Construction Material Industrial Corp., Vice General Manager of Zhejiang Dongfang Group Corp., General Manager of Zhejiang Dongri Co., Ltd. Currently, he is General Manager of Zhejiang Dongfang Group Corp., Chairman of Zhejiang Dongri Group Co., Ltd., Vice President of Zhejiang Entrepreneur Association and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Qian Jinbo, male, born in September 1964, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be General Manger of Yongjia Aolin Shoes Co., Ltd., General Manger of Yongjia Hong Qing Ting Shoes Company, Chairman and President of Zhejiang Hong Qing Ting Group Co., Ltd., Chairman and President of Hong Qing Ting Group Co., Ltd. Currently, he is Chairman of Hong Qing Ting Group Co., Ltd. and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Li Yuping, male, born in February 1962, master degree and senior economist, used to be Manager of the Sales Department of Yongjia Foreign Trade Company, Chairman of Sanhu Concrete Group Co., Ltd., and Chairman of Natural Real Estate Development Group Co., Ltd. Currently, he is Chairman of the Board Committee of Natural Real Estate Development Group Co., Ltd. and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Shao Fenghua, male, born in November 1972, junior college degree, used to be Vice Chairman of Sanhu Concrete Group Co., Ltd. Currently, he is Chairman of Sanhu Concrete Group Co., Ltd., and Director of Bank of Wenzhou

Ying Shude, male, born November 1964, undergraduate degree, used to be Chairman of Wenzhou Tianxiong Property Development Co., Ltd., Chairman of Zhejiang Education, Technology and Cultural Development Co., Ltd., Chairman of Shanghai Shude Investment Co., Ltd., Chairman of Wenzhou Overseas Study Service Center, and Chairman of Shanghai Caoyang High School. Currently, he is Chairman of Shude Group and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zhou Dingwen, male, born in March 1963, junior college degree, used to be Deputy Head of the Urban Area of Yueqing, Vice Director of Agriculture and Economic Commission of Yueqing, Director of Yueqing Forestry Bureau, Executive President of Wenzhou New Century Group Co., Ltd., Chairman of Wenzhou New Century Real Estate Company. Currently, he is Chairman of Wenzhou Jiahong Real Estate Development Co., Ltd. and Director of Bank of Wenzhou.

##### (ii) Supervisors

Chen Xiwen, male, born in January 1958, senior economist and taking a Master program at present, used to be Vice Director of the Planning Department of ABC Wenzhou Branch, Vice Director of the General Planning Department of the People's Bank of China Wenzhou Branch, Director of Finance Administration Department of the People's Bank of China Wenzhou Branch, Vice Executive Director and Vice Director of Wenzhou Urban Credit Cooperatives Central Office, Deputy Director of the Incorporation Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, Vice President and Director of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Chief Supervisor of the Supervisory Board of Bank of Wenzhou.

Song Yongsheng, male, born in December 1948, undergraduate degree and senior administrator, used to be Vice Manager of Dongtou Kelp Breeding Farm, President of ICBC Wenzhou Branch. Currently, he is External Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.

Xiong Jizhou, male, born in October 1963, Ph.D., professor and doctoral student supervisor, used to be Manager of Yinchuan Transformers Factory, Secretary of Department of Public Finance of Ningxia, Vice Director of General Office and Chief of the Information Center, Chairman, Vice President, Executive Vice President and Deputy Secretary of Yinchuan City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Professor of Finance, doctoral student supervisor and Secretary General of the Financial Research Institute of Fudan University and External Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zhang Yaohui, male, born in August 1965, EMBA degree and associate professor, used to be Associate Professor in Accounting at Jiangxi University of Finance and Economics, Manager of China Economic Development Trust & Investment Co., Ltd. Nanchang Division, General Manager of Hengdian Group Hi-Tech Industrial Co., Ltd., and General Manager of Hengdian Group Co., Ltd. Currently, he is Chairman of Harbin Hi-Tech (Group) Co., Ltd., Chairman of Zhejiang Henqxinqli Holdings Group Co., Ltd. and Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.

Fei Lianyou, male, born in February 1950, junior college degree and administrator, used to be work at China Navy East China Sea Marine, Publicity Department of Shanghai Base, Political Department of East China Sea Marine, Political Department of Navy 37622 in Zhoushan Base of East China Sea Marine, and Director of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Vice Director of Wenzhou Tobacco Monopoly Bureau and Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.

Zheng Xiangbo, male, born in March 1957, college degree and economist, used to be Director of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Chairman of Taili Industrial Co., Ltd. and Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.



Wang Yameng, female, born in August 1964, undergraduate degree and economist, used to be Director of Business Handling Center and General Manager of Personal Banking Department of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, she is General Manager of Accounting and Settlement Department of Bank of Wenzhou and Staff Supervisor.

Lv Limin, female, born in June 1963, undergraduate degree, economist and administrator, used to be Director of the Personnel and Security Department, Vice Director of the General Office and Vice Chairman of the Labor Union of Conch Group, Chief of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank Daqiaotou Sub-Branch Hongchang Banking Office, Director of the Credit and Loan Department of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank Fulong Sub-Branch, Vice General Manager (supervising work) of the Special Assets Management Department of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, she is General Manager of Special Assets Management Department of Bank of Wenzhou and Staff Supervisor.

Lin Shaocong, female, born in September 1955, junior college degree and economic specialist, used to be Director of the Personnel Department of the 2nd Hemp Spinning and Weaving Mill of Wenzhou, Director of Saving Office of Wenzhou Huiyuan City Credit Cooperatives, Assistant of Manager of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank Da'nan Sub-Branch, and Vice Director of the CCP (Labor Union) Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, she leaves her position to relax. She is Staff Supervisor of Bank of Wenzhou.

Hong Jianjie, male, born in December 1970, master degree and economist, used to be Assistant of President and Vice President of ICBC Wenzhou Dongtou Sub-Branch and Wuma Sub-Branch, General Manager of the Credit and Loan Management Department of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, General Manager of Risk Management Department and Assistant of President of Bank of Wenzhou. V. Changes in Directors, Supervisors and High-Rank Managerial Staff of the Company

On January 31, 2008, the 21st meeting of the Board of the Company nominated Jin Jiankang and Hong Jianjie Vice President.

### VII. Staff

As of the end of the reporting period, the Company has 1,503 employees on the payroll, including 380 managerial staff members, 1102 frontline staff members and 21 administration staff members. In the employees in service, 41.12% of them have undergraduate degree or above; 48.24% have junior college degree; and 10.64% have below junior college degree.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2319_200_2383_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


# ☐ VII.Corporate Governance Structure

## I. Corporate Governance

The Company strictly observed the Rules of the Company Act of the P.R.C. and the Law of Commercial Banks of the P.R.C., and carried out the requirements of the regulatory authorities on corporate governance. Taking account of the actual condition of the Company, the Company promoted the corporate governance system construction structured based on the Board, the Supervisory Board and the management, and optimized the corporate governance structure of which decisions made by the Board, operation management by the Office of President and supervised by the Supervisory Board. The regulatory level of corporate governance was improved effectively.

During the reporting period, the Company amended the Articles of Incorporation of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd., the Detailed Rules on the Work of President, the Detailed Rules on the Work of the Nomination and Compensation Committee of the Board, the Detailed Rules on the Strategic Development Committee and the Detailed Rules on the Work of the Audit Committee of the Board, formulated the Detailed Rules on the Work of the Secretary of the Board and further optimized the basic rules for corporate governance, providing system assurance for the normative operation of the corporate governance of the Company.

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' meeting

During the reporting period, the Company held the 1st extraordinary shareholders' meeting of 2008 and the annual shareholders' meeting of 2007 in line with the requirements of the Articles of Incorporation. The meetings passed 24 important proposals including Work Report of Board and Report on Business Plan Fulfillment in 2007 and Business Plan for 2008. The 1st extraordinary shareholders' meeting of 2008 and the annual shareholders' meeting of 2007 respectively hired Beijing Tianyin Lawyers Office and Zhejiang Renmin United Lawyers Office to be present at the meeting for witness and provide attorney's opinion letters.

##### (ii) Directors and Board of Directors

As of the end of the reporting period, the Board had 18 members, including 1 chairman, 1 vice chairman, 6 independent directors, 9 shareholder directors, and 1 management director. During the reporting period, the Board carried out the financial policies of the government seriously, effectively implemented the regulatory opinions of the banking regulatory authority and decided on important business affairs properly. Targeted to optimize corporate governance, the Company further promoted the operation of the Shareholders' Meeting, the Board, the Supervisory Board and the management, reinforced the core management function of the Board, enhanced the construction of internal control system and total risk management, kept standardizing the operation of the Board, fulfilled the functions and responsibilities, further deepened first-level corporate system and fully exerted the guiding and decision-making role of the Board.

During the reporting period, the Board held 10 meetings in total, discussed 81 proposals and adopted 61 resolutions.

During the reporting period, the Board made and optimized the development plan for 2008–2010, setting out three strategic goals: to develop from a small bank to a mid-sized bank, from a local bank to a regional bank and from a traditional bank to an innovative bank. The Board hired Guoxin Securities Co., Ltd., Tianyin Lawyers Office and Lixin Certified Public Accountants to be the nominated advisor, issuer's lawyer and auditor respectively, formulated basic rules such as the Management Rules on Investor Relation, and actively promoted the IPO counseling; set up new organizations in important cities in Zhejiang, deepened financial services in Wenzhou and actively played the strategic guiding role. The Board initiated total risk management strategies, amended the Articles of Incorporation of the Company, made the Three-Year Plan for Information Technology Development, released Instructive Opinions on Total Risk Management, analyzed and assessed the risk status of the Company regularly, and reinforced the management of transactions with related parties to improve the risk controlling ability constantly. The Board optimized the construction of the internal control system, kept dealing with specific cases and assessed internal control regularly to improve the role of internal control management. The Board reinforced multi-level authorization management, optimized information transmission system, reinforced the construction of the Board, improved the quality of information disclosure, kept enhancing internal governance system, promoted the balance development of efficiency, quality and scale with effective corporate governance and promoted the construction of modern commercial bank constantly.



##### (iii) Supervisors and Supervisory Board

The Supervisory Board has 9 members, including a chief supervisor, 2 external supervisors, 3 shareholder supervisors and 3 staff supervisors. By holding regular meetings, reviewing various documents of the reports of the Company, listening to the work report and special reports of the management, participating in internal control examination and assessment, the Supervisory Board examinee and supervised the business status, financial activities and internal control and risk management. During the reporting period, the Supervisory Board hired intermediary agency to audit the financial and business management status, internal control and risk management and coordinated the meetings of the regulatory department, the intermediary agency and the Company. The Supervisory Board supervised the quality of credit assets, main regulatory indicators and risk control status, gave supervisory opinions regularly, participated in the internal post-loan management examination, specialized case handling and comprehensive social security improvement programs. Meanwhile, the Supervisory Board communicated and gave opinions and advice to the top management for the problems found in supervision and audit.

During the reporting period, the members of the Supervisory Board attended 2 shareholders' meetings, attended 5 board meetings as a non-voting participant and 20 president administration meetings as a non-voting participant to supervise the legality and conformity of the shareholders' meeting, the board and the top management, the voting procedures and the conformity and duty performance of the directors and top management members. During the reporting period, the Supervisory Board also assessed the duty performance of the directors, supervisors and top management members.

The Audit Committee communicated with Lixin CPAs concerning the audit of 2007 and audited and analyzed the business management status, internal control and risk management of the Company in 2007.

#### (iv) Top Management

In 2008, the Company held 21 president administration meetings to study and discuss 120 proposals including

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


total risk management system frame and the articles of association of the risk control committee, personnel and organization setup plan, cooperating with city commercial banks in Zhejiang to No.0801 Jia Zhi Lian Cheng Wealth Management Plan, launching fund sales agent service, cooperation plan with banks and commercial chambers in other cities, allocating lobby manager and wealth management center, setting up a marketing department under the headquarters, setting up wealth management office, compensation and allowance adjustment, purchase of real properties and construction projects. The top management held 18 specialized meetings to study and made the development plan for 2008–2010, the business plan for 2008 and the financial budget for 2008 and made important deployment for the important information system's emergency drill for Olympic Game. The top management held 3 bank affairs meetings, bringing up a series of plans and approaches for carrying out various regulatory opinions and resolving the difficulties and problems in the development of the branches and sub-branches. In 2008, the management fulfilled the duties and obligations according to the Company Act of the People's Republic of China and the Articles of Incorporation, carried out various resolutions of the Shareholders' Meeting and the Board, promoted business transformation, speeded up product innovation, reinforced risk internal control and improved management ability. The business performance and asset quality improved greatly. The top management fulfilled business tasks successfully. The overall strength and social influence of the Company kept improving.

##### (v) Information Disclosure and Transparency

According to the requirements on information disclosure, the Company improved the timeliness, accuracy and completeness of information disclosure and ensured all shareholders accessed to the information equally. During the reporting period, the Company finished the preparation of the Annual Report of 2007 in time in line with the Information Disclosure Management Rules and published the Summary of the Annual Report of 2007 in Financial News. The Company also provided the Report in the Office of the Board and branches for investors and related interested persons to consult. Meanwhile, the Company handled mails, calls, visits and questions of shareholders seriously to safeguard their, especially minor and middle investors' interests.

## II. Duty Performance of Independent Directors and External Supervisors

The Company has 6 independent directors on the Board and 2 external supervisors who take office of chief on various specialized committees. During the reporting period, the independent directors and supervisors organized, held and attended the meetings of the Board, the Supervisory Board and various specialized committees. They expressed their opinions actively and played effective roles as independent directors and external supervisors.

(iii) Objections to matters of the Company raised by independent directors

## III. Independent Operation of the Company

The highest power body of the Company is the Shareholders' Meeting. It manages and supervises business operation through the Board of Directors and the Supervisory Board. The President is hired by the Board of Directors and takes full charge of the daily business management of the Company. The Company adopts first-level corporate system. All branches are non-independent accounting units. Their business activities are operated under the authorization of the headquarters and they report to the headquarters.

The Company does not have any controlling shareholder and actual controller. As a commercial bank, the Bank keeps totally independent in business, personnel, assets, organization and finance under the regulation of the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission. The Company is an independent corporation which runs business independently and is entitled or bear the gain of loss of the Company with sole responsibility. The Company has independent and complete business and independent business operating ability.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2319_201_2383_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


## IV. Assessment and Incentive System for High-Rank Managerial Staff

During the reporting period, the assessment indicators for the top management included if he fulfilled the decisions of the Board and the fulfillment of the business goals given by the Board and if he actively maintained the interests of shareholders and retained and increased the value of the assets of the Company. With 13 indicators in four categories, i.e., business development, risk control, business performance and business operation conformity, the assessment result, rewarding and penalty were closely related to the compensation. Meanwhile, Company's top management accepted the management of the Board and the supervision of the Supervisory Board and reported to the Board and the Supervisory Board on the financial status and business outcomes of the Company to ensure directors and supervisors to be informed about the daily business operation and management of the Company constantly.

The incentive and restrictive system of the Company was embodied in the assessment and compensation system of the high-rank managerial staff. According to the Articles of Incorporation, the compensation policy and assessment system are determined by the Board. The fulfillment of the business goal of the management, the rewarding plan for over-fulfilling profit and the management rules on the performance assessment of the top management were reported to and approved by the Nomination and Compensation Committee under the Board and finally determined by the Board.

## I. Annual Shareholders' Meetings

The annual shareholders' meeting of 2007 of the Company was held at Onehome Howard Johnson Hotel Wenzhou on April 30, 2008. Eighty-five shareholders and authorized representatives of the shareholders were present at the meeting, holding 854,344,171 valid voting shares, accounting for 83.50% of the total shares of the Company. The meeting discussed and passed 7 proposals, i.e., Work Report of the Board, Development Plan for 2008–2010 of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd., Report on Business Plan Fulfillment in 2007 and Business Plan for 2008, Final Accounting Report of 2007 and Financial Budget Report of 2008, Report on Final Accounting of Profit Distribution for 2006, Report on Profit Distribution for 2007, and Work Report of the Supervisory Board. The meeting circulated 3 reports, i.e., the Report on Carrying out Regulatory and Examination Opinions of 2007, Report on the Implementation of Rules on Management of Transactions with Related Parties and Transactions with Related Parties in 2007, and Duty Performance of Directors, Supervisors and Top Management of 2007.

## ☐ VIII.Shareholders' Meeting

## II. Extraordinary Shareholders' Meeting

The 1st extraordinary shareholders' meeting of 2008 was held at Onehome Howard Johnson Hotel Wenzhou on January 31, 2008. Eighty-five shareholders and authorized representatives of the shareholders were present at the meeting, holding 854,379,451 valid voting shares, accounting for 83.50% of the total shares of the Company. The meeting discussed and passed 14 proposals, i.e., Proposal on Application for IPO of the Company, Proposal on Application for IPO and Listing of the Company, Proposal on Investment Projects with Fund Raised by IPO of the Company, Proposal Submitted to the Shareholders' Meeting for Authorizing the Board of the Company to Handle Matters of IPO of the Company, Proposal on Distribution of Undistributed Profit of the Company before IPO, Proposal on the Provision of General Reserve, Proposal on Share Registration and Trusteeship of the Company, Proposal on Amending the Articles of Incorporation of Bank of Wenzhou, Proposal on Amending the Rules on Discussion of Shareholders' Meeting, Proposal on Amending the Rules on Discussion of Board of Directors, Proposal on Amending the Rules on Discussion of Supervisory Board, Proposal on Amending the Work Rules of Independent Directors, Proposal on Amending the Detailed Rules on the Management of Bank of Wenzhou's Transactions with Related Parties and Proposal on Formulating the Management Rules on Fund Raised.

##### (i) Scope of main business

## II. Discussion and Analysis of the Management

Approved by the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission, the main business of the Company includes: receiving public deposit; issuing short-term, mid-term and long-term loans; handling domestic and overseas settlement; handling bill acceptance and discount; issuing financial bonds; issuing, cashing and selling government bonds as an agent; buying and selling government bonds and financial bonds; operating inter-bank borrowing and lending; deposit, loan and remittance of foreign currencies; exchange of foreign currencies; international settlement, purchase and sale of foreign currencies; purchasing and selling or acting as an agent to purchase and selling foreign currencies; operating bankcard business; providing L/C service and guarantee; authorized collecting and paying funds and selling insurances as an agent; providing safe box service, operating E-Banking business; and other businesses approved by the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission.



##### (ii) Overall business development

In 2008, the Company kept persisting in the market positioning of based on local economy, based on small and middle enterprises and based on residents in cities and countryside, aimed to be an outstanding retail bank and followed modern commercial bank operating mode to improve management, run business prudently, reformed aggressively and was enterprising and innovative. The Company actively adjusted asset structure and customer structure, focused on business expansion and risk management, overcame the negative influences of financial crisis and macroeconomic regulation and realized constant and steady growth in various businesses. The details are as follows:

1. Steady growth in overall business scale. The total assets at the end of the year was 34.141 billion yuan, increasing 4.058 billion yuan, or 13.49%, compared with that at the beginning of the year, in which, the balance of various loans was 20.479 billion yaun (including discounts), increasing 3.337 billion yuan, or 19.47%, compared with that at the beginning of the year. The total liabilities was 32.341 billion yuan, increasing 3.679 billion yuan, or 12.84%, in which, the balance of various deposits was 27.658 billion yuan, increasing 4.797 billion yuan, or 20.98%, compared with that at the beginning of the year and the income from intermediary business was 54,496,300 yuan, increasing 27,235,100 yuan, or 99.90%, compared with that in the previous year.

2. Profitability kept improving. The Company realized business revenue of 1.241 billion yuan for 2008, increasing 324 million yuan, or 35.33%, compared with that in the previous year; total profit of 593 million yuan for 2008, increasing 89 million yuan, or 17.66%, compared with that in the previous year, after-tax net profit of 466 million yuan, increasing 125 million yuan, or 36.66%, compared with that in the previous year, and owners' equity is 1.8 billion yuan, increasing 380 million yuan, or 26.76%, compared with that of the previous year.

3. Operating cost lowered effectively. The Company reinforced expense conformity supervision and made great efforts to lower operating cost. The intensive operating ability and overall efficiency were improved significantly. The income/expense ratio reduced by 1.85%.

4. Capital adequacy ratio improved effectively. At the end of the reporting period, the capital adequacy ratio of the Company was 10.60%, increasing by 0.99% compared with that at the beginning of the year. The core capital adequacy ratio was 7.43%, increasing by 1.14% compared with that at the beginning of the year.

5. Non-performing loan increased. Affected by negative factors including financial crisis and macroeconomic regulation, the non-performing loan in 2008 increased. The balance of the five-class non-performing loan was 297.90 million yuan, increasing 170.18 million yuan compared with that at the beginning of the year. The non-performing loan ratio was 1.45%, increasing by 0.70% compared with that at the beginning of the year. The provision coverage ratio was 110.13%, decreasing by 73.82% compared with that at the beginning of the year. The adequacy ratio of allowance for loss on loan was 143.20%, increasing by 41.74% compared with that at the beginning of the year.

6. The distribution of banking institutions was optimized and cross-regional development was fruitful. During the reporting period, the Company made more efforts in setting up new banking institutions and adjusting existing institutions. The Company finished the relocation of Binhai, Xinqiao and Yongjia Sub-Branch and Wencheng Sub-Branch opened for business successfully, becoming the first institution of the Company established by setting up

a new institution in Wenzhou. Ningbo Branch opened on a trial basis on December 28, indicating the cross-regional development of the Company had made a critical step.

7. Social influence kept increasing. During the reporting period, various regulatory indicators of the Company met the regulatory requirements and the Company was rated second class in regulatory rating. The overall strength and market competitive strength improved remarkably. The Company was ranked the 18th in the 2008 Board of Overall Competitive Strength of City Commercial Banks in China released by the Banker, and was selected one of the three Best Big City Commercial Banks' in the 2008 Board of Best Financial Institutions in China organized by Chinese Academy of Social Science.

#### (iii) Operation of Main Business

1. Corporate banking business: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of ordinary corporate deposit was 17.137 billion yuan, accounting for 61.96% of the total deposit of customers, increasing 1.436 billion yuan, or 9.15%, compared with that at the beginning of the year. The balance of loan to corporations was 10.185 billion yuan, accounting for 49.73% of the total loan to customers, increasing 2.816 billion yuan, or 38.21%, compared with that at the beginning of the year.

2. Personal banking business: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of personal deposit was 10.521 billion yuan, accounting for 38.04% of the total deposit of customers, increasing 3.36 billion yuan, or 46.92%, compared with that at the beginning of the year. The balance of loans to individuals was 10.294 billion yuan, accounting for 50.27% of the total deposit of customers, increasing 521 million yuan, or 5.33%, compared with that at the beginning of the year.

3. Intermediary business: During the reporting period, the Company realized revenue from intermediary business of 54.50 million yuan, increasing 27.22 million yuan, or 99.78%, compared with that in the previous year. The Company issued 778,700 debit cards, increasing 75,300 cards compared with that at the beginning of the year. The monthly average balance per card was 4,480 yuan, increasing 796 yuan compared with that at the beginning of the year. The Company issued 28,000 credit cards (active card), increasing 14,300 cards compared with that at the beginning of the year. The Company realized revenue from bankcard business of 20 million yuan (excl. credit card interest income).

VI. Changes in major accounting policies and accounting estimates or major accounting errors of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company did not have any non-raised fund investment project.

During the reporting period, the Company did not have any changes in major accounting policies and accounting estimates or correction of major accounting errors.

VII. Notes to the choice of major accounting policies and explanation for important accounting estimates

Based on continuing operation and according to the actual transactions and events, the Company recognized and measured transactions in line with Enterprise Accounting Principles– Basic Principles and other accounting principles (hereinafter the New Principles or the New Accounting Principles) published by the Ministry of Finance in February 2006 and prepared financial statements based on it.

The Company adopted accrual basis accounting system to recognize, measure, report transactions. Tradable financial assets and available-for-sale financial assets were recorded at their fair value. The rest was recorded at their historical cost. In the reporting period, the measurement property of the reporting items kept unchanged.

Transactions in different foreign currencies were recorded separately. The Company prepared financial statements in RMB at the end of the period. The principle of preparing financial statement in RMB was to prepare statements in different currencies at first, then convert the currencies to USD and then convert USD to the amount in RMB and prepare the financial statement in RMB.

## VIII. Material Asset Loss Caused by Joint Liability of the Company due to Guarantee Provided for Outside Parties

During the reporting period, the Company did not have any joint liability due to guarantee provided for outside parties.

## IX. Debts of the Company, Changes in Counseling and Cash Arrangement for Repaying Convertible Bond in the Future

As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of subordinated bond issued by the Company was 550 million yuan. The Company has sufficient ability to repay the principal and interest of the subordinated bond.

## X. Major Changes in Business Environment and Macroeconomic Policies and Regulations and Their Influences

#### (i) Macroeconomic regulation

In 2008, the CPI in China soared in the first four months and then went down. It touched the highest level in April and then kept decreasing. The inflation expectation weakened. But the expectation of economic turning down grew. Since early 2008, the central bank increased statutory deposit reserve ratio for six times. Because of the changes in the macroeconomic environment, the central bank began to reduce the statutory deposit reserve ratio in September. The statutory deposit reserve ratio was reduced for four times till the end of 2008. Meanwhile, the central bank had reduced benchmark interest rate for five times since September. The market interest rate fluctuated widely in 2008.

Facing with the increasing macroeconomic regulations, the Company controlled loan issue reasonably, kept steady and balanced loan growth, further adjusted asset structure, ensured and restricted loan issuance properly, optimized loan structure, increased types of consumer loan, and expanded consumer loan business. Meanwhile, the Company made efforts to secure intermediary agent business and wealth management business to look for new profit generators. Generally, the macroeconomic regulation accelerated the Company's asset structure and income structure adjustment and did not impose material negative influence to the Company.

##### (ii) Deposit and loan interest rate adjustment

September 16, 2008, the People's Bank of China reduced loan benchmark interest of financial institutions. The one-year loan benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 7.47% to 7.20%. Other loan interest rates were also adjusted accordingly. The deposit benchmark interest rate kept unchanged.

October 9, 2008, the People's Bank of China reduced deposit and loan benchmark interest of financial institutions. The one-year deposit benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 4.14% to 3.87%. The one-year loan benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 7.20% to 6.93%. Other deposit and loan interest rates were also adjusted accordingly.



October 30, 2008, the People's Bank of China reduced deposit and loan benchmark interest of financial institutions. The one-year deposit benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 3.87% to 3.60%. The one-year loan benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 6.93% to 6.66%. Other deposit and loan interest rates were also adjusted accordingly.

November 27, 2008, the People's Bank of China reduced deposit and loan benchmark interest of financial institutions. The one-year deposit benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 1.08% from 3.60% to 2.52%. The one-year loan benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 1.08% from 6.66% to 5.58%. Other deposit and loan interest rates were also adjusted accordingly.

December 23, 2008, the People's Bank of China reduced deposit and loan benchmark interest of financial institutions. The one-year deposit benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 2.52% to 2.25%. The one-year loan benchmark interest rate of financial institutions reduced 0.27% from 5.58% to 5.31%. Other deposit and loan interest rates were also adjusted accordingly.

To the rise of deposit and loan interest rate, the Company prepared for it timely and completely. On the one hand, it reinforced asset and liability management, optimized asset structure and increased income from loan business. On the other hand, it effectively prevented interest rate risk. As the interest rate got higher gradually, the Company shortened investment duration. In general, the central bank's lowering deposit and loan interest rate for five times reduced the net interest difference a little, but had no big influence on the business performance of the Company. In the future, the Company will keep tracing, analyzing and studying interest rate risk and emphasize asset and liability management.

##### (iii) Statutory deposit reserve rate adjustment

The People's Bank of China adjusted statutory deposit reserve rate ten times on January 25, March 18, April 25, May 20, June 15, June 25, September 25, October 15, December 5 and December 25. The statutory deposit reserve rate rose from 14.5% to 17.5% for the first six times and reduced from 17.5% to 13.5% (small-to-mid-sized financial institutions) and 14.5% (big financial institutions) for the last four times. The main purpose was to reinforce and improve macroeconomic regulation and liquidity management, control the growth of total money and loan to a proper degree and maintain a good economic development trend.

The central bank's constant increase of statutory deposit reserve rate brought new challenges to the liquidity management of the bank. It required the management team to tighten liquidity management and keep adequate liquidity and profitability. As the Company had adequate fund source and unblocked financing channels, the increase of statutory standby deposit had limited influence to the liquidity of the Company and did not imposed

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


material influence on the liquidity management of the Company. The Company adjusted the proportions of high-liquid assets such as investment, financing and bills and actively participated in bill market, monetary market and bond market to ensure property liquidity and improve profitability.

##### (iv) Changes in foreign exchange rate

In 2008, the People's Bank of China carried out foreign exchange management system reform in full scale. The RMB exchange formation mechanism was gradually optimized. The basic role of market demand and supply became increasingly important. Under the circumstances of international financial crisis, the RMB exchange rate changed widely. In the first half year, the exchange rate rose from 7.2996:1 to 6.8591:1. The currency appreciation rate was as high as 6.4%. In the second half of 2008, the exchange rate changed the unilateral appreciation tendency and adjusted between 6.80:1 and 6.87:1. It closed at 6.8346:1 at the end of the year, only appreciating by 0.4%. RMB appreciated by 6.8% over the year. Analyzing the tendency over the year, the independence, balance and flexibility of the exchange rate of RMB improved significantly and the two-way fluctuation character became more obvious.

To respond to the risk caused by the changes in the exchange rate, the Company enhanced the ability to identify, measure and supervise exchange rate risk and optimizing the internal risk control system while emphasizing the management over exchange rate open. According to the market exchange rate fluctuation, the Company adjusted asset liability structure in time.

##### (v) Changes in home loan policy

In 2008, the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission released the Notice of Further Reinforcing Management of Credit Approval for the Real Estate Industry (YJF [2008] No.42) and the Notice of Finance Promoting Economical and Intensive Use of the Land of the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission (YF [2008] No.214). These policies are positive to the healthy development of the real estate industry and regulate the real estate credit risks of commercial banks. According to the notice, to reinforce and improve property loan management, control real estate credit risks and promote constant and healthy development of the property loan business of the Company, the Company Amended the Detailed Rules on Real Estate Loans of Bank of Wenzhou and released the Detailed Rules on the Management and Operation of Loans to Government-Aided House Development of Bank of Wenzhou to instruct the operation of real estate loan business. The Company carried out related policies strictly and reinforced examination and audit on real estate loan business. Through the above approaches, the Company maintained high asset quality while keeping the property loan business developing healthily.

## XI. Difficulties in business operation and solutions

##### (i) Difficulties in business operation

During the reporting period, the Company encountered the following problems and difficulties in business: ① With the spread of the international financial crisis and the increasing operation risk of national economy, China's economic development slowed down, the capital chain of enterprises tightened and the potential risk of the financial sector was increasing. ② The profitability of the traditional business of the bank kept decreasing. The innovative ability of local commercial banks was weak. Products and functions were incomplete and market competitiveness needed to be improved. ③ China's macroeconomic regulation policy became more flexible. The pace of exchange rate liberality was getting faster, setting out higher requirements on the top management of the Company in liquidity risk management ability and the ability of responding to market changes. ④ The Company lacked high-rank managerial staff and professionals. The personnel structural problem became more obvious and the overall competence of the staff needed to be improved.

1. The Company improved total risk management ability and optimized credit asset structure. By releasing the Instructive Opinions on Total Risk Management of Bank of Wenzhou and Articles of Incorporation of the Risk Control Committee, setting up Risk Control Committee and developing liquidity pressure test system, the Company improved liquidity management ability and built the total risk management frame. Meanwhile, the Company actively carried out macroeconomic regulation policy, further optimized credit structure, kept developing new credit products to satisfy the demand of the market and customers and launching new credit products to optimize financial services for small business.

##### Aiming to the above problems, the Company focused on the following work:

2. The Company made more efforts in product innovation and accelerated the development of intermediary business. The Company optimized the features and services of the credit card and invented installment payment for overdraft from cash withdrawal, creating a new income generator in the credit card business. The Company initiated personal wealth management product innovation. The first personal wealth management product "No.0801 Jia Zhi Lian Cheng Wealth Management Plan" was sold in 9 city commercial banks in Zhejiang. The Company signed cooperation agreement with Ji Li Fu Company and actively worked on the application for the qualification for selling fund as an agent and system development.

3. The Company optimized internal management and reinforced conformity construction. By improving business supervision and IT controlling ability, carrying out conformity risk assessment and general business self-examination and internal control and conformity examination alike, business supervision was developed from manual operation to electronic process. It enhanced the conformity operation in the business process and effectively ensured operation security.

4. The Company adjusted organization setup and created cross-regional business structure. The Company made efforts in setting up new banking institutions and adjusting existing institutions. Wencheng Sub-Branch opened for business successfully, becoming the first institution of the Company established by setting up a new institution in Wenzhou. Ningbo Branch opened on a trial basis on December 28, indicating the Company's

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_367.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


##### (ii) Outlook and Approaches

1. The development trend of the banking industry, market competition situation and influence on the Company

In 2009, the national economy will keep developing as the expectation of the macroeconomic regulation. The pace of economic growth will slow down moderately, the economic structure will show positive changes and the financial sector will operate safely and steadily. The banking industry are favored with strategic opportunities as the central government actively expands domestic demand, promotes the reform and development in countryside, guarantees the steady development of SMEs and the regional economy transforms and upgrades.

The active non-state-owned economy of Wenzhou and good financial environment are highly appealing to all banks. With the financial industry further opening up, the market competition in the region will be fiercer, mainly seen in the following aspects: ① national joint-stock commercial banks race to grab the market of Wenzhou. ② stock-joint banks and non-state-owned capital begin to compete for the market shares in the regional developed counties and important towns. ③ joint-stock banks and city commercial banks begin to compete for the financial resources in the under-developed regions in Zhejiang. ④ the economic integration in Yangtze Delta and the building of the half-day transportation circle of Yangtze Delta speed up the information flow, capital flow and human resource flow in the region. Big city commercial banks in Yangtze Delta are renamed one after the other and start cross-regional operation, indicating the competition for financial resources and HR resources in a bigger area will keep increasing.

The year 2009 will be a special year that China's economic structure will have in-depth adjustment and China will carry out positive financial policy and moderate loose monetary policy. It will also be a critical year for the Company to create cross-regional business structure and promote business transformation and upgrade. The Company will catch development opportunities from changes, discover and develop favorable factors, speed up the adjustment and transformation of business management, actively respond to the challenges brought by increasing competition and financial risk, and realize strategic, leaping, healthy and fast development.

2. Development opportunities and challenges in the future concerned by the management of the Company

(1) Opportunities

① With the constant healthy development of China's economy and the accumulation of wealth of residents, the financial industry will be booming. As a new force, city commercial banks will make great achievements.

② The regional economy of Wenzhou will keep steady and fast development, providing a good regional financial environment (low bad loan rate, good credit awareness and normative business behavior) for the development of the banking industry.

③ The successful IPO of Bank of Ningbo, Bank of Nanjing and Bank of Beijing opened a new external financing channel for city commercial banks. The Company will catch the good opportunity to launch IPO and try to list in the A Share market so as to improve capital power and business scale quickly.

④ The complete opening up of the banking industry provides a good opportunity for the industry to carry out international cooperation and introducing in foreign-funded strategic partners. It will promote city commercial banks to innovate in the management system, services and products and human resource development.

⑤ Cross-regional business operation brings the best coordination between regional expansion and business expansion. It will help city commercial banks find new and suitable market positioning in the process of transformation.



##### (2) Challenges

① Affected by the financial crisis and macroeconomic turning down, the biggest challenge for commercial banks is to keep good asset quality and constant profit growth. ② From the point of competition in the banking industry, the development of the financial industry will show cross-country, cross-industry, cross-institution and cross-market new characters. The business form of the financial industry will be diverse. The financial resources will be concentrated. The competition in the industry will be fierce. The companies in the financial industry will invest more in resource and speed up cross-regional business operation. Full-scale competition and differentiated competition will co-exist. Financial competition will escalate and small and mid-sized banks will go through a survival test. ③ From the point of Bank of Wenzhou itself, the Company has been restricted by the bottleneck of sustainable development and extrinsic development. The business system, internal control management and the construction of the staff team are unsuited and unmatched with healthy and fast development. It sets out higher requirements on the Company's transforming development strategies, changing business growth mode and realizing scientific development.

Due to the increasing uncertainty in macroeconomic development, the system risk and credit risk of the banking sector shall not be neglected. The increasingly reinforced capital regulation puts more pressure on small and mid-sized commercial banks' supplementing capital. Under the circumstances that traditional business mode has no substantial change and financial disintermediation, the profit ability of the Company tends to be affected by the increasing credit cost and decreasing interest difference. The monetary policy is adjusted frequently and the interest rate market reform is accelerated, bringing new challenges to the Company's ability to resist economic fluctuation and control financial risks.

## XII. Business Development Plan for 2009

##### (i) Guideline for business development of 2009

Facing the new opportunities and challenges brought by social and economic development, the Company will persist in the principle of "controlling risk as the first priority and developing steadily", be motivated by successful IPO, center on structure adjustment and adopt positive approaches to prevent various risks. The Company will catch opportunities to optimize business structure, strengthen weak parts, enhance reform,

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_367.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


innovation and technology progress, improve business management ability, lay a solid foundation, improve competence and realize the sustaining and steady development of various businesses.

##### (ii) Business goal for 2009

Various deposits increases 6 billion yuan over the year and the balance at the end of the year reaches 33.627 billion yuan. Loan increases 4.5 billion yuan over the year and the balance at the end of the year reaches 24.978 billion yuan. The loan/deposit ratio over the year is controlled below 75%. At the end of the year, the capital adequacy ratio reaches over 10% and core capital adequacy ratio over 6%. Realize total profit of 633.10 million yuan in 2009.

##### (iii) Main approaches for business development in 2009

① Create long-acting capital supplement system to ensure capital adequacy ratio up to the standard.

② Promote management system reform and explore the headquarter, branch and sub-branch three-level management structure.

③ Accelerate the construction of process banking and create a vertical operation management system.

④ Accelerate the pace of business transformation and improve market competitive strength.

⑤ Improve the idea of independent innovation and create characteristic brand of the Company.

⑥ Deepen economic capital management and optimize target assessment system.

⑦ Carry out total risk management and establish long-acting internal control system.

⑧ Build the staff team and optimize the compensation and allowance system.

⑨ Exert technology's leading and supporting effect and further improve IT application level.

⑩ Reinforce the construction of the management team and keep improving the ability to lead technology development.
