<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td rowspan="2">Items</td><td colspan="3">Balance at the beginning of the year</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Amount</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Related pledge amount</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Pledge percentage (%)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>L/C issued</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5,050,692.85</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>15,369,588.79</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>304.31%</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>L/G issued</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>42,064,085.30</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>23,639,607.84</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>56.20%</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Bank acceptance bill issued</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10,558,756,212.39</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5,528,488,850.31</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>52.36%</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loan undertaking (credit card)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>313,310,333.18</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr></table>

L/C is a credit loan in which the Bank is requested and instructed by the applicant to issue a written guarantee letter of certain amount which guarantees to pay the amount in a given period at a given place upon given documents.

Bank acceptance bill is a credit loan business in which the payee or payer (or cashing applicant) issues a commercial acceptance bill, the cashing applicant applies to the Bank and the Bank agrees to cash the commercial acceptance bill after examination.

L/G is a credit loan business in which the Bank is requested by the applicant or authorized person to issue a letter of guarantee promising to the beneficiary that when the applicant fails to fulfill the liabilities and undertakings set in the contract, the Bank will cover the debt or bear the liabilities as ruled in the letter of guarantee.

Loan undertaking refers to the Bank promises to the customer to give a certain amount of credit line in a certain period after the Bank discusses with the customer and issues a letter of undertakings.

# 立信會計師事務所有限公司 Shu Lun Pan Certified Public Accountants Co., Ltd.

Internal Control Attestation Report

All Shareholders of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd.:

XKSBZ (2009) NO.22122

We were entrusted to audit the effectiveness of the internal control related to the financial statement as of December 31, 2008 involved in the attached Self-Assessment Report on Internal Control of the Bank prepared by the Board of Directors of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. (hereinafter the Bank).

The Board of the Bank is responsible for establishing and optimizing the internal control and keeping its effectiveness. We bear the responsibility to give our opinions on the attestation of the effectiveness of the internal control of the Bank.

The internal control has intrinsic limitation and the possibility of false statement and undiscovered false information due to errors or fraudulent practices. Besides, the circumstance changes may cause internal control improper or lower the degree of internal control following the controlling policy and process. It has some risks to use the internal control result to speculate the effectiveness of the internal control in the future.

We carried out the attestation in line with the rules of Principle No.3101 for Other Attestations of China CPAs—Attestations other than Audit or Review of Historical Financial Information. The principle requires us to plan and carry out the attestation in order to gain proper evidence indicating if the Bank had no material error in all material aspects in the assessment on the effectiveness of the internal control report related to the financial statements. During the attestation, we learned, tested and assessed the rationality of the design of the internal control and the effectiveness of the implementation of the internal control and carried out other procedures we believed necessary. We believe out attestation provided reasonable basis for our opinions.

In our opinion, the criteria set by the Bank according to its self-assessment report on internal control kept effective internal control related to the financial statement as of December 31, 2007 in all material aspects.

# Board of Directors of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. Self–Assessment Report on Internal Control of the Bank

1. Internal Control Environment

### (i) Governance Structure

Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. (hereinafter the Bank) has optimized a corporate governance structure making up of Shareholders' Meeting, Board of Directors, Supervisory Board and the top management. The Bank has specific committees including Strategic Development Committee, Risk Management Committee, Audit Committee, Committee of Related Party Transaction Control, Nomination and Compensation Committee, and Technology Information Committee, etc. and the Office of Board under the Board. Under the Supervisory Board, there is Nomination Committee, Audit Committee and the Office of Supervisory Board. The Board, the Supervisory Board and the various specific committees under them formulated the rules on business discussion and process for decision-making. Each governance subject is able to exercise its responsibilities in line with its duties and rules and process.

#### The governance structure of the Bank is as follows:

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1334_793_2240_1470.jpg" alt="Image" width="37%" /></div>


<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2319_200_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


The Board is responsible for the establishment, optimization and effective implementation of the internal control system; planning for overall business strategies and major policies and checking and assessing the implementation regularly; ensuring the Bank to operate business prudently in the legal and policy frame and setting acceptable risk degree; ensuring the organization structure frame of the Bank; and ensuring the top management to supervise and assess the completeness and effectiveness of the internal control system, in which:

1. The Strategic Development Committee makes the general business operation and development strategies for the Bank and gives related advice.

2. The Risk Management Committee is responsible for the risk management policy and related advice and participates in the management of major risk matters.

3. The Committee of Related Party Transaction Control manages the related parties of the Bank, examines the recognition of the related parties and examines and approves material related transactions.

4. The Nomination and Compensation Committee selects, examines, advises the members of the Board and the top management and assesses their performance.

5. The Audit Committee supervises, assesses the internal control of the Bank and feeds back information.

6. The IT Management Committee is responsible for specific information technology development strategies, gives related suggestions and participates in the management of major information system projects.

2. The Audit Committee is responsible for related intermediary agency's audit of the business position of the Bank.

The Supervisory Board supervises the Board of Directors and the top management to optimize the internal control system; supervises the members of the Board of Directors, the top management and high-rank managerial staff to fulfill the responsibilities of internal control; and prevents the directors, the chairman and high-rank managerial staff damaging the interests of the Bank and supervises implementation, in which:

1. The Nomination Committee of the Supervisory Board is responsible for examining the qualification of the nominees for supervisors and gives suggestions.

Upon the authorization of the Board, the top management formulates the internal control policy, supervises and assesses the completeness and effectiveness of the internal control system; carries out the decisions of the Board; recognizes, measures and supervises related risks and controls the risks; establishes and optimizes the internal organization structure and ensures the effective fulfillment of all responsibilities of the internal control.

#### (ii) Organization Structure

According to the requirements on internal control of the Bank, the functional departments of the Bank fall into three categories as marketing department, risk control department, and operation and support department to separate front-end marketing, mid-end risk control and back-end support and assurance for various businesses. The following aspects have been realized:

1. The position investigating credit and the position examining and approving credit are separated.

2. The position examining and approving credit is separated from account processing.

3. Centralized management of financial accounting.

4. Centralized management of over-the-counter settlement business.

5. System development and system operation are separated.

#### (iii) Internal Control Policies

According to laws, regulations and regulatory requirements and taking into account of the reality of the Bank, the Bank has established an internal control system based ISO9000 quality management system. It covers the internal control system for the management activities of various businesses of the Bank, falling into ten categories as corporate governance, credit approval business, credit card business, international business, capital business, financial accounting and settlement, human resource, technology, internal audit, examination and assessment, and administration, support and others.

##### (iv) Company culture construction

The Bank is creating common values to instruct staff to deal with the relation between personal interest and collective interest and the relation between collective interest and social interest. The values will influence the development of the Bank, guide and regulate the behavior mode of the staff, and motivate their sense of responsibility and sense of master. Meanwhile, the Bank is developing fair, clean, law-abiding, service and diligent professional ethics of the staff, which encourages the staff to be enterprising, hardworking and disciplined, and urge the staff to obey the law and regulations. The Bank is making efforts to create good company spirit and company image.

#### (v) Human resource policy

The Bank is establishing a market-oriented HR management system, carrying out appointment and compensation system reform following the liberal market principle and optimizing staff incentive system. The Bank recruits and hires personnel, decides pay and assesses performance according to the positions, establishes incentive system and training system for different levels of staff so as to create a fair play environment and career development for the staff.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_201_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


## II. Risk Identification and Control

The Bank has established Risk Management Department to exercise risk management function. The Bank has started formulating the rules, process and methods to identify, measure, monitor and manage risks and recognized and managed major risks such as credit risk, operating risk, legal risk and ethical risk. The Bank is setting up related process and models to identify and measure market risk and liquidity risk. The top management of the Bank is able to identify and control risks in time and sends the information to the decision makers via regular reports and irregular brief reports. The Bank has set up a new frame for the internal control of risk management according to the requirements on the internal control of commercial banks set by the Banking Regulatory Commission. The Bank has provided adequate capital for all risks of the Bank according to the requirements on capital adequacy ratio of commercial banks set by the Banking Regulatory Commission. The Bank is establishing a complete and reliable management information system for the measure, supervision and control of market risk and credit risk and taking actions to ensure the data correct, reliable, in time and safe.

## III. Controlling Approaches for Main Business

The departments of the Bank have formulated complete rules and regulations on daily business activities including credit approval business, capital business, intermediary business, foreign exchange business, and over-the-counter business. The process of each business has defined authorization and approval and can be reported to the financial accounting and related departments for recording, checking and filing the original vouchers.

#### (i) Control over credit approval business

1. Established Risk Management Committee and Committee of Related Party Transaction Control and the decision-making system for credit management.

2. Separated loan examination and loan approval to enhance the credit risk control capability.

3. Optimized customer assessment system and credit rating system, formulated Rules on Corporation Credit Rating Management and Rules on Management of Loan Quality Monitoring and Early Warning Reporting

4. Established credit approval, credit line management and risk monitoring management system; set authority limit for each sub-branches and signed letter of re-authorization with them. Within the authority limit, the sub-branch has the right to approve loans and make payments. For loans out of the authority, the Credit and Loan Approval Department of the Headquarters will examine and approve the loan and the Fund Release Center will release the fund in a centralized manner.

5. Established credit approval business management and credit approval risk control over group customers and related–party customers, formulated Rules on Risk Management of Centralized Credit Approval for Group Customers and Implement Rules on Management of Transactions with Related Parties.



6. Created and carried out the five-degree loan risk system.

7. Created collateral assessment system under credit approval business.

8. Established early risk warning system for credit approval business to reinforce risk control over credit approval business.

9. Established credit approval risk responsibility system and specified the risk responsibilities of each department and each position.

10. Established and carried out non-performing loan assets responsibility investigation system, reinforced responsibility discipline and specified the responsibilities of the credit approval positions.

11. The credit approving positions had reasonable work division and clear responsibilities. Different positions were cooperative and inter-restrictive. Loan issuance and approval were separated. Business processing and accounting were separated.

12. Each organization specified the authority and work process of credit examiner and credit approver to examine and approve business strictly under the authority and process.

13. On-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet credit approval including loan, trade financing, bill acceptance and discount, overdraft, factoring, guarantee, loan undertaking, L/C issuance for single customer was controlled under a total amount.

14. Established unified credit approving operation rules to specify the work standards and due diligence requirements on each process including pre-loan-issuance investigation, loan issuance examination and post-loan-issuance examination.

15. Formulated unified management rules for various credit products and specified the conditions for each business.

16. Followed the principle of confirming the condition before approving credit when the condition was allowed. If the condition for approving the credit is not met or the condition is changed and the new decision is not made, the credit shall not be approved.

17. Carried out independent due diligence investigation for credit approval. Decision on credit approval was made in line with set process. During the decision-making process of credit approval, the staff approving the credit must follow objective and fair principle and give decision-making opinions independently.

18. Examined and monitored the purpose of the loan; examined the validity, financing background of the borrower and the authenticity of the application documents and completeness of the loan contract to prevent the borrower swindling loan or doing other financial frauds.

19. Established asset quality monitoring system to monitor the change of asset quality, discover the potential risk of asset quality in time, analyze the reasons for the formation of non-performing asset and work out countermeasures preventing and resolving the risk in time.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_201_2380_368.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


20. Established credit management information system to monitor the full course of credit approval constantly in order to provide true credit business status and asset quality information and analyze the credit risk and return comprehensively.

21. Established customer management information system to learn the financial standing, business and financial status, solvency and non-financial factors of the customer in a complete and centralized manner. Managed customers in different categories and prohibited borrowers of bad financial standing.

22. According to the risk level, specified different approving authorities for credits of different types, terms and guarantee conditions. Both qualitative and quantitative methods were used in credit approval. Tried to quantify risk indicators to improve the operability of the business.

##### (ii) Control of capital business

1. Adopted unified credit approval, multi-level authorization and centralized management for capital business.

2. The development and operation of new capital business products must be approved by the authorization of the top management.

3. Optimized the internal control for capital operation. Transferred in and out capitals according to the true business background, operated business strictly according to the authority and allocated the fund and entered transaction diary in time.

4. The operation, approval and accounting of capital business were separated. The front-end trade and back-end settlement were separated. Business operation and risk monitoring and control were separated.

5. Gave necessary assessment on the risks and loss of capital business. Bond transactions were set stop-loss point. When the loss exceeds the stop-loss point, the operator must take action to stop loss. Borrowing and lending between other banks were set risk alarm line.

6. Formulated capital business operation norms and checked it on a regular basis.

7. Accounted for capital business in a centralized way and checked transaction diary and accounting books in time.

8. Established a proper restrictive system for capital traders to manage capital traders effectively.

9. Established the reflection and supervision system of the mid-stage and back-stage departments for the front-stage trade.

10. Established capital trade risk assessment indicator system to assess the returns and risks of capital trade in time and prudently in order to control all risk indicators of capital business within the set scope.

11. According to the risk degree and management ability of the capital trade, grant authority to the capital trader for trade product, trade amount and stop-loss point.

12. Calculated the market value and floating gain or loss of the trade position according to the market price and monitored the market risk and the change in the market value of the position of the capital trade products in real time.

13. Established internal reporting system for the risk and market value of capital trade and specified the sending scope, process and frequency for reports of different levels and types.



14. Established pressure test process to simulate and estimate the possible potential loss caused by incidents such as sharp changes in market price or unexpected political and economic events so as to assess the Bank's ability to bear the loss under extremely adverse conditions.

#### (iii) Control of intermediary business

15. Established risk responsibility system of capital business and regulated the risk responsibility of every depentment and position definitely.

1. Established management rules and operation norms for various intermediary businesses to prevent operation risk.

2. Established responsibility system for each position. Specified the responsibilities of each position and established accountability system to determine the responsibility of the risks caused by illegal acts and acts breaking the rules.

3. Checked the business data with accounting data and statistic data for off-balance-sheet items.

4. In purchasing, selling foreign currencies and making payment in foreign currencies, the responsibilities of approving, operating and account bookkeeping were separated. Strictly carried out internal management and examination rules to ensure the purchase, sale of foreign currencies and payment in foreign currencies conforming to the regulations.

5. In handling authorized business, the Bank sets separate accounts for the transactions and optimized the payment, collection and check process of authorized capital to avoid authorized capital to be used or embezzled and ensure specific fund for specific purpose.

6. Examined and managed authorized fund payment and transferred fund according to the agreement of the authorization agreement. Followed the principle of not paying on behalf of the client and not involving in trade disputes between the client and others.

7. Strictly calculated and recognized the income of various authorized businesses according to the accounting system. The authorized receipt and payment were separated to avoid income from authorized business to be retained or embezzled.

#### (iv) Control of bankcard business

1. The Bank opened account under real name in issuing debit card. For cash withdrawal, account transfer and spending payment of debit card, the Bank formulated and carried out related management rules and operation process.

2. The Bank established effective monitoring system for the overdraft behavior of credit card holders. The business processing system had real-time monitoring, excess amount control and stopping payment for abnormal transactions. The Bank checked the account with the credit card holders on a regular basis, strictly managed overdraft amount to prevent vicious overdraft risk.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_201_2380_367.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


3. The Bank set necessary monitoring approaches for capital transaction of bankcard when it dealt with bankcard deposit, withdrawal and account transfer business to prevent the card holder used the bankcard for illegal activities.

4. The Bank managed appointed stores of bankcard, regulated related operation process and processing procedures and formulated corresponding emergency and preventive approaches for the business risk and operation errors of appointed stores.

##### (v) Control of over-the-counter business

1. Set settlement management position to manage over-the-counter settlement business of the Bank. Grant system authorities for various businesses and personnel in a centralized manner to control internal risk.

2. Made related operation rules to prevent operation risk.

3. Separated the management of the seal, test key and voucher. The staff using and keeping important business seals was not allowed to keep related business vouchers. The staff using and keeping test key was not allowed to use or keep related seals or vouchers to prevent operation risk and internal crimes.

4. Carried out post-transaction supervision system for over-the-counter business, which ensured the separation of processing staff and supervising staff.

6. Established standards, reporting process and responsibility investigation system for major mistakes in overt-the-counter business.

5. Implemented multi-leveled authorization and double-signature system for the issue and withdrawal of big-amount deposit certificate and recorded and reported the receipt and payment of big-amount fund to ensure the information of big-amount transactions true and complete so as to prevent money laundry, financial fraud and illegal activities alike.

7. Strictly carried out the rules on account management to check the identity of the depositor and the authenticity, completeness and legality of the account information. Checked account opening, change and cancellation regularly to avoid depositors to rent or lend out accounts or use the accounts to do illegal activities.

8. Managed the reserved seals and deposit payment vouchers; improved the ability to identify if the seals and vouchers were real and used computer technology to enhance the technology content in the management of reserved seals and prevent frauds.

9. Carried out effective management for deposit accounts; established and optimized transaction diary and account checking system to check the accounts between the Bank and customers, the Bank and other banks and within the Bank. Specified the frequency, object and participants of the account check.

10. Monitored special internal account transfers and abnormal account changes and tracked and analyzed unusual account changes.

11. Effectively managed the account records at the end of the workday. The vouchers of the day were recorded on the day. Carried out internal approving and recording process for wrong bookkeeping and untaken bills or returned bills.



12. Established checking system for cash receipt and payment, fund transfer, change of account information, change of password, loss reporting and cancellation of loss reporting to ensure transaction records complete and traceable.

13. Carried out strict accounting and management for cash, precious metals, important blank vouchers and value bills; followed the procedures for the storage, recording and taking out of the above items and check the storage on a regular basis.

## IV. Internal Control of Accounting System

(i) The accounting rules and management system of the Bank was formulated in line with the enterprise accounting principles.

(ii) Defined the independence of accounting work and the accounting department and accounting personnel being able to process accounting independently according to China's unified accounting system of the accounting rules of the Bank.

(iii) The setup of accounting positions followed the principle of separating responsibilities and restricting each other. It was strictly banned to allow one person to work in two incompatible positions or finish the entire business process alone.

(iv) Defined the authorities of the accounting department and accounting personnel. The accounting department in different levels and accounting personnel worked within their authorities. Business beyond the authority should be processed after being authorized.

(v) Supervised the entire process of account processing. Accounting records, accounting records and vouchers, accounting records and funds, accounting records and facts, accounting records and financial statements and internal and external accounts shall be accordant.

(vi) Adopted professional qualification management for accounting supervisor and person in charge of accounting. Established files of accounting personnel. Hired accounting supervisor, person in charge of accounting and accounting personnel who have the professional qualification or skills for the position.

(vii) The accounting supervisor of the lower–level organization was assigned by the upper–level department. The transfer or resignation of accounting personnel shall finish handover procedure.

(viii) Established compulsory leave system for accounting personnel. Personnel in important accounting positions and accounting supervisors changed positions on a regular basis. Adopted leaving–position audit system..

(ix) Adopted investigations system for accounting errors. If serious accounting error, malpractice or case occurs, besides investigating the responsibility of the directly responsible person, the person in charge of the accounting department and the person in charge of accounting shall bear related responsibilities.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_201_2380_367.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


(x) Accounting records and account processing were legal, true, complete and correct.

(xi) Established normative information disclosure system and disclose accounting and financial information in time, truly and completely to satisfy the information demand of shareholders, the regulatory authority and the public.

(xii) Gradually optimized accounting file management and adopted accounting file consultation procedures to prevent accounting files being changed, altered, damaged, missing or secret being disclosed.

## V. Control of Computer System

At present, the computer systems used by the Bank include general business system, credit management system, credit card system, international business system, online banking system, financial management system and office automation system. The main controlling procedures of the computer systems include:

(i) Formulated position responsibility system to make clear the position responsibilities of system developers, managerial staff and operators of the computer information system. It was not allowed to take two positions so that different positions could restrict each other. Meanwhile, the Bank assigned computer security management staff to reinforce the security management of the computers.

(ii) Managed the entire process from the project establishment, development, inspection and acceptance, operation and maintenance of the computer information system. The development environment and production environment were separated to ensure the security of the system.

(iii) While buying computer software and hardware, examined the qualification of the supplier and performed security test before use. Defined the responsibility of the supplier during the period when the product was in use so as to ensure the normal use and effective maintenance of the product.

(iv) The construction of the computer room was in line with related governmental standards. Entering and leaving the computer room had strict approving procedure and kept records to ensure the physical safety of computer hardware and various storage media.

(v) The computer room and banking offices were equipped with computer monitoring system to ensure the normal use of technology equipment.

(vi) The Bank is taking actions to establish and optimize the network management system to manage the safety, malfunctions, performance and configuration of the network. Carried out security management for internet access.

(vii) User management and password management were adopted in the computer information system to control the creation, change, deletion of the user and the length and valid period of the user's password so as to avoid the possibility of operation risk and internal crimes.

(viii) Established proper authorization process for the access to the information system and took post-access security control to ensure the security of the computer system.

(ix) Updated system security settings, virus source code database, attack feature codes, software patches, etc. Adopted technology approaches including certification, encryption, content filtering and intrusion monitoring to ensure the safe operation of the computer information system.



(x) Set necessary logs for the operation system, database system and application programs.

(xi) Managed various kinds of data information and formulated management rules on data operation, the storage, transfer and destroying of data backup media to ensure data information secure.

(xii) Online banking service had customer ID identification and security certification features to avoid the leakage of secrets.

(xiii) Established computer security emergency system and formulated emergency plan. Revised the plan and drilled on a regular basis. Data backup was kept in another place. Established computer disaster backup center.

(xiv) Prevented various operation risks and illegal acts and crimes by making the most use of the system settings of the computer information system

## VI. Supervising and Correcting System

(i) The Internal Audit Department of the Bank is an audit department specifically for internal audit. It is authorized to monitor and assess all departments, positions and businesses.

(ii) Made the rule that the Internal Audit Department could report to the Board of Directors or the Audit Committee directly.

(iii) The Internal Audit Department had qualified internal auditors. Established professional training system. Every person must have certain time of off-position or full-time training every year.

(iv) Established the reporting and information feedback system for internal control. The business department, internal audit department or other controlling staff were able to report to the Board, the management or the related department when they found hidden problem or defects in internal control.

(v) The Internal Audit Department examined and assessed the system construction and implementation of the internal control, gave advice on improvement and suggested on how to punish organizations and personnel breaking the rules.

(vi) The upper–level organization assessed the internal control performance of the lower–level organization on a regular basis according to the internal control information it had and made the assessment result an important basis for business performance assessment.

(vii) Established the punishing and corrective system for internal control problems and defects. The management brought up rectifying opinions and corrective approaches according to the internal control examination and assessment result and urged the business department and branches to carry them out.

(viii) Established the internal control risk responsibility system.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_2320_200_2380_367.jpg" alt="Image" width="2%" /></div>


#### (i) Weaknesses in internal control

1. In the governance structure of the Company, the office of the secretary of the Board was taken by the top management. The Bank did not have a full-time secretary of the Board. The due diligence of some members of the Board and the Supervisory Board should be improved.

2. In HR policy, because of the fast-growing business and tight staffing, staffing in some positions was unable to fully satisfy the requirements of internal control. The assessment system laid emphasis on business development. The Internal management system, restrictive system and incentive system need to be reinforced.

3. In risk measurement and assessment, though risk identification and assessment had cover all businesses, the Bank was weak in risk model measurement, especially, the tightness and effectiveness of the system, process and method in risk identification, measurement, monitoring and control need to be improved.

4. In credit approval management, the rules were not fully carried out. The normalization and conformity in business activities need to be improved. Bill discount business had not been included in the comprehensive credit management system.

6. The data warehouse system had not been constructed and commercial intelligence application had not been carried out. The support for management decision making needs to be improved. The security of the information system and the ability to prevent various emergencies and vicious attacks need to be improved.

5. In accounting and budget management and control, the Bank had not adopted operating cost analysis, capital transfer pricing, asset and liability management and profit analysis application systems. The controlling ability and business reflecting ability of internal management accounting need to be improved.

7. The Bank had not established a special and systematic new product development system and did not have systematic, definite and specific requirements for the prediction before launching a product and the assessment after launching the product.

##### (ii) Corrective actions

To the above weaknesses, the Bank has taken actions, made policies and process to improve the internal control system. At present, all corrective actions are under progress:

1. Keep promoting the system construction for the Board of Directors and the Supervisory Board. Reinforce assessment and training and further improve the due diligence of the members of the Board and the Supervisory Board. Select a proper person to take the office of secretary of the Board and work in full time.

2. According to the business development plan, actively hire mid-rank and high-rank financial personnel. Reinforce occupational training and optimize assessment system. Under the precondition of the unification of safety, efficiency and performance, optimize HR allocation and satisfy the needs of important positions such as capital, credit management and counter management to ensure HR and business development suitable for internal control.

3. The Bank is actively working out the approaches integrating the resources of the Bank and the external power and structuring related risk model measurement to improve the ability of identifying, measuring, monitoring and controlling various risks. Meanwhile, the Bank keeps updating ISO 9000 Quality Management System and internal control system to reinforce risk identification, measurement and control.



4. Reinforce the executive force for the credit approval system. Reinforce conformity education and the examination and supervision of the effectiveness of carrying out the system so as to improve the internal control ability in credit approval.

5. The Bank has planned to introduce in or develop an advanced bank cost control and performance analysis tool. It will be planned and carried out in near future in order to improve accounting management ability.

6. Actively carry out effective technology information strategy. By catching the opportunity of restructuring various business processes and information technology application, improve the security of the technology information system and the ability dealing with emergencies and vicious attacks. Based on information technology application, optimize the new product development system.

7. The Bank is reinforcing the construction of the new product development system based on promoting IT application. Regulate new product development from the planning for new product development strategies, making development plan, designing for development process, supervising the development process and verifying the product result so as to improve the ability of new product development management.

In conclusion, the Bank has established the internal control system covering all businesses, meeting the requirements on internal control of commercial banks. The system plays an important role in enhancing business management, controlling business risk and preventing malpractices. It guarantees the Bank's assets safe and integral, protects the rights and interests of interested parties related to the Bank, and improves the credit standing and market competitive strength of the Bank.
